289 ~ // /// 📜️ The `.wires` textual DSL and op-text grammar are declared, not hand-rolled — see the
290 ~ // /// `#[derive(dsl::DslDocument)]` on `MindmapWiresDocument` and `#[derive(dsl::DslOps)]` on
291 ~ // /// `MindmapWiresOperation` in `🔖️Document`/`🔖️Operations` above. Both `wires_fixture`/`board_fixture`
292 ~ // /// (and the `Value`/`serde_json::Map<String, Value>` operation payload fields) bind directly through
293 ~ // /// `dsl`'s built-in `Shape::Value` escape hatch for opaque/freeform JSON (see `dsl/rs/lib.rs`) — no
294 ~ // /// local mirror type or hand-rolled tokenizer needed. This region intentionally holds no additional
295 ~ // /// code; the generated `impl vcs::DocumentDsl for MindmapWiresDocument`/`impl vcs::OpText for
296 ~ // /// MindmapWiresOperation` live entirely in the derive expansion.
297 ~ // ///
298 ~ // /// 🕸️ The unified `a:Kind@port->b@port` wire syntax (`dsl::Wire`/`Shape::Wire`) does NOT apply here:
299 ~ // /// edges live inside the opaque `board_fixture`/`wires_fixture` `Value` trees (plain JSON objects with
300 ~ // /// `source`/`target` string fields), not as typed Rust fields a `#[dsl(...)]` attribute could target —
301 ~ // /// that's the whole point of keeping this crate free of board-engine schema types (see the struct doc
302 ~ // /// above). Introducing a wire-literal encoding for those JSON edges would mean hand-rolling a bespoke
303 ~ // /// sub-printer for one field shape inside the generic `Shape::Value` escape hatch, and the same
304 ~ // /// `source`/`target` JSON shape is shared by every other generic board/map fixture in the repo
305 ~ // /// (`reasoning.mindmap.fixture`, tiled-map, puzzle boards, ...) — a structural, cross-crate schema
306 ~ // /// change out of scope for this pass. `metabolism.wires` is confirmed to round-trip unchanged under
307 ~ // /// the unified engine (see `dsl_round_trip_metabolism_fixture` below).
    |
